We show that the spaces of normalized $\surd$-max simple random variables form a monad over the category of $T_0$ spaces and that the spaces of normalized $\surd$-max continuous random variables give a monad over the category of d-spaces. In addition, on a sober space, the space of normalized $\surd$-max continuous random variables is the sobrification of the space of normalized $\surd$-max simple random variables.

</details>


### [96] [Undecidability of Confluence for Binary Length-Reducing Cycle Rewriting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18859)
*Graham Campbell*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文证明：即使循环重写系统在固定二元字母表上、每条规则都严格缩短且右端非空，合流性仍不可判定，并且是Π⁰₁-完全的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线性字符串与项重写中，有限终止系统的合流性可通过临界对分析判定；但图重写系统的合流性不可判定。本文研究介于两者之间的循环字符串（字符串按旋转视为等价），揭示仅由旋转操作引入的复杂性。

Method: 首先将确定性验证器编译为带有唯一受控分支的加权循环重写系统；随后利用游程编码将其转换为二元、长度严格递减的循环重写系统。额外的清理规则保证所有可约的格式错误循环都归约到同一个错误范式，从而保持目标合流性问题的计算复杂度。

Result: 在固定字母表{0,1}上，有限循环重写系统的合流性是Π⁰₁-完全的，即使每条规则右端非空且严格缩短。由于长度严格下降，非空长度为n的循环至多经历少于n步归约，因此终止性在语法上显然。该结论推广到任意至少含两个字母的固定字母表；单字母情形则可判定。

Conclusion: 字符串按旋转取商这一看似简单的结构变化，足以使合流性从可判定转为不可判定。因而，终止性和局部长度约束并不能恢复循环重写系统的合流性判定性，字母表规模则表现出明确的复杂度分界。

Abstract: Confluence guarantees that diverging rewrite choices can always be rejoined. For finite terminating string- and term-rewriting systems, confluence is decidable by critical-pair analysis, and in polynomial time for length-reducing strings. For finite terminating (hyper)graph transformation systems, in contrast, confluence is undecidable. We show that undecidability already appears for words on a circle, that is, strings up to rotation. Confluence of finite cycle-rewriting systems over the fixed alphabet $\{0,1\}$ is undecidable, indeed $Π^0_1$-complete, even when every rule has a nonempty right-hand side and strictly reduces length. Under this restriction termination is syntactically evident, and derivations from a nonempty length-$n$ cycle have fewer than $n$ steps. The same holds over every fixed alphabet with at least two letters, while the one-letter case is decidable. Rotation alone separates cyclic from string rewriting. The proof compiles a deterministic verifier into a weighted cycle system with one controlled branch, then into a binary length-reducing system via a run-length code whose cleanup rules send every reducible malformed cycle to one error normal form.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [97] [Demo: tfdrift - A Severity Taxonomy and Risk Classification Framework for Infrastructure Drift Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18173)
*Sudarshan Bhagvanthakur*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出面向基础设施即代码（IaC）配置漂移的风险分级方法，并在开源工具 tfdrift 中实现。该方法通过按资源类型和属性变更影响划分四级风险，在降低告警量的同时尽可能保留重要变更。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Terraform 等 IaC 工具虽能声明式管理云资源，但实际部署状态与声明状态之间仍会发生配置漂移。现有检测机制通常对所有变更等量告警，导致告警疲劳，使运维人员难以及时关注高风险修改。

Method: 作者构建通用的漂移严重性分类体系，依据资源类别及具体属性级变更的影响，将漂移划分为四个风险层级；随后在 tfdrift 框架中实现超过 60 条可配置规则，覆盖 AWS、Azure 与 GCP 的资源模式。实验主要聚焦 AWS Terraform 工作区，并通过风险级别过滤对告警进行筛选。

Result: 在超过 150 个 AWS Terraform 工作区上的评估显示，严重性过滤可将告警数量减少 73%，同时保留 94% 的安全相关变更。结果表明，基于规则的轻量级分类能够有效缓解告警过载，并可作为机器学习告警过滤方案的替代或补充。

Conclusion: tfdrift 将配置漂移检测从“所有差异同等处理”转变为基于影响的风险优先级处理。其主要贡献是可配置、跨云的规则化严重性分类框架；不过，摘要中的实证结果仅集中于 AWS，其他云平台的实际有效性仍需进一步验证。

Abstract: Infrastructure as Code (IaC) tools like Terraform have become the standard for declarative cloud resource management, yet configuration drift, where deployed infrastructure diverges from its declared state, remains a persistent operational and security challenge. Current detection approaches treat all changes equivalently, contributing to alert fatigue that causes operators to miss security-critical modifications. We propose a generalized severity taxonomy for infrastructure drift that classifies changes into four risk tiers based on resource type and attribute-level impact. We implement this taxonomy in tfdrift, an open-source classification framework with 60+ configurable rules covering AWS, Azure, and GCP resource patterns (evaluation reported here is AWS-focused). Evaluation across 150+ AWS Terraform workspaces demonstrates that severity filtering reduces alert volume by 73% while retaining 94% of security-relevant changes, offering a lightweight alternative to ML-based alert filtering. tfdrift is available at github.com/sudarshan8417/tfdrift.

</details>


### [98] [Reproducibility is Not Enough: Artifact Verifiability in Decentralized-Build Package Ecosystems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18180)
*Oreofe Solarin,Kelechi Kalu,James C. Davis,Paschal Amusuo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文系统评估四个去中心化构建软件包生态中制品能否被独立复验，发现可验证性不仅依赖确定性构建，更受源代码、构建环境、依赖与发布流程元数据缺失的显著制约。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式软件制品的可信性需要第三方能够独立确认制品是否确由声明的源代码和流程生成。但去中心化生态中的工具链、维护者工作流与元数据格式高度异构，导致现有研究尚不清楚大规模制品实际可复验的比例及主要障碍。

Method: 作者定义仅使用包注册表可推导元数据的独立验证者模型，并提出分层制品等价性比较模型；随后实现制品验证流水线，在四个主流去中心化构建包生态中测量制品可验证性，并归因影响验证结果的构建与元数据因素。

Result: 实验表明，即使构建本身具有确定性，验证仍常因缺少源状态、构建环境、依赖信息和构建指令而失败。隐式发布转换、非标准构建实践也会削弱复验能力。来源证明与制品内嵌版本控制系统元数据能够改善验证条件，但仍不足以提供完整、可重建的构建规格。

Conclusion: 要在包注册表规模上实现实用的制品验证，生态系统需要补足可获取的源代码与构建元数据，显式记录发布阶段转换，并规范化构建与依赖描述。论文的主要贡献是给出可操作的独立验证模型、测量流水线及针对生态级改进的具体缺口分析。

Abstract: Reproducible and verifiable builds increase trust in distributed software artifacts by enabling independent parties to detect artifacts produced by compromised build or release pipelines. However, artifact verification requires more than deterministic builds: a verifier must also recover the source state, build environment, dependencies, and build instructions that produced the artifact. Decentralized-build ecosystems make this difficult because artifacts are produced through heterogeneous tools, maintainer-controlled workflows, and fragmented metadata. As a result, it remains unclear how often artifacts in these ecosystems can be independently verified.
  This paper studies artifact verifiability across four popular decentralized-build package ecosystems. We define an independent verifier model that relies only on registry-derivable metadata and an artifact comparison model with tiered equivalence levels. We implement these models in an Artifact Verification Pipeline and use it to measure artifact verifiability across the target ecosystems. Our results show that, beyond build determinism, verifiability is limited by missing source and build metadata, implicit release transformations, and unconventional build practices. Provenance attestations and embedded VCS metadata improve verification, but they do not provide complete rebuild specifications. These findings identify concrete metadata gaps and ecosystem-level changes needed to make artifact verification practical at package-registry scale.

</details>


### [99] [What Makes Software Issue Resolution Tasks Difficult for Agents?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18280)
*Ebtesam Al-Haque,Brittany Johnson*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出面向软件问题修复任务的难度测量框架，并在大规模编程智能体轨迹数据上验证：仅依赖任务的静态属性即可较准确预测智能体是否成功完成任务，预测AUC为0.863。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有编程智能体评测基准趋于饱和，但分数缺乏可解释性，因为任务难度通常未被控制或刻画。论文旨在回答哪些任务结构属性导致难度差异，以及能否在执行前根据静态信息预测难度。

Method: 作者在CoderForge-Preview数据集上开展大规模实证研究，从补丁、代码仓库和任务提示三个层面提取特征；利用集成学习预测任务结果，并结合SHAP归因和效应量分析，评估各类静态特征与成功率之间的关联及贡献。

Result: 静态特征对任务结果具有较强预测力，AUC达到0.863。最关键的难度来源是补丁碎片化程度和仓库规模：修改越分散、项目规模越大，任务通常越难。对于难度处于中间区间的任务，提示文本的语言学特征也进入重要贡献因素，表明任务难度具有分层结构。

Conclusion: 软件问题修复任务的难度可由其结构性属性编码，并能够在任务执行前进行估计。该发现可支持构建按难度分层或受控的评测基准，从而提高编程智能体评估结果的可解释性与可比性。

Abstract: Background. Advances in agentic systems are simultaneously, and rapidly, saturating benchmarks. Despite this often discussed phenomena, benchmark scores remain difficult to interpret due to the lack of control and characterization of task difficulty. More specifically, we currently have little understanding of what makes one task harder than another, and to what extent task difficulty is predictable from static task properties. Aims. We propose a measurement framework to investigate and systematically quantify what structural properties of software tasks correspond to agent success rates for issue resolution tasks. Method. We conducted a large scale empirical study on CoderForge-Preview, the largest open dataset of coding agent trajectories to date, by extracting features across task patch, repository and prompt. We evaluated the predictive power of each feature against task outcomes using ensemble methods, SHAP attribution, and effect size analysis. Results We found that task difficulty is substantially predictable from static features (AU C = 0.863) and is largely driven by patch fragmentation and repository scale. Prompt linguistic features become visible among top contributors for tasks in the mid-band, revealing a layered structure of difficulty. Conclusion. The difficulty of an issue resolution task is encoded in its structure. This enables static, pre-hoc difficulty estimation and lays the groundwork for difficulty-controlled benchmark construction for evaluation of agents.

</details>


### [100] [Engine-Transfer-Bench: An Evidence-Based Benchmark for Document Compilation Engine Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18329)
*Prajwal S. Venkateshmurthy*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出Engine-Transfer-Bench（ETB），用于系统比较pdfLaTeX、XeLaTeX、LuaLaTeX、Tectonic、Typst及pandoc PDF后端在跨平台文档编译中的可靠性、延迟、文本一致性与失败模式，并发布基准、推荐器和可移植性检测工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文档编译引擎的选型缺乏统一、可复现的比较框架。不同操作系统、发行版策略和依赖供给方式会显著改变编译成功率，使仅基于单机经验的选型结论不可靠。

Method: 作者构建含1,784份开放文档的ETB，设置可靠性、延迟、文本一致性和失败分析四类任务；使用固定版本的测试环境，在macOS、Ubuntu和Windows上执行每主机4,211次编译；另从中识别702份可移植LaTeX文档，并以50对样本验证S_pdf文本一致性指标。

Result: Tectonic在不同主机上的成功率稳定在96.3%至97.2%，跨平台波动不超过0.9个百分点；传统TeX Live风格引擎受Ubuntu apt、MiKTeX自动安装和macOS BasicTeX等发行策略影响，成功率可相差12至20个百分点。在702份可移植LaTeX文档中，受测引擎均达到100%成功率，因此延迟成为主要决策因素。失败集中于107个引擎特定模板，主要涉及字体、版式和资源文件等架构性兼容问题，而非已充分配置主机上的缺包问题。S_pdf对真实内容差异的判别精度为94%。

Conclusion: ETB表明，编译引擎选择应同时考虑文档可移植性、宿主环境策略和性能，而不能仅依据引擎名称或理论兼容性。对于已验证可移植的文档，优先按延迟选择；对于异构或跨平台工作流，应利用ETB的推荐器、可移植性门禁及跨操作系统测试框架提前发现字体、布局和资源相关的不兼容。

Abstract: There is no shared framework for selecting among document compilation engines (pdfLaTeX, XeLaTeX, LuaLaTeX, Tectonic, Typst, and pandoc PDF backends). We present Engine-Transfer-Bench (ETB): 1,784 open documents, four tasks covering reliability, latency, text consistency, and failures, a pinned harness, and host-tagged multi-OS results. On GitHub Actions (N=4,211 compiles per host across macOS, Ubuntu, and Windows), Tectonic success is stable within 0.9 percentage points (96.3-97.2%), whereas classic TeX Live-style engines vary by 12-20 percentage points according to distribution policy (Ubuntu apt, MiKTeX auto-install, or macOS BasicTeX). On 702 portable LaTeX documents, the tested engines succeed at 100%, making latency the primary selection factor; failures concentrate in 107 engine-specific templates. Within ETB, failures are architectural, involving fonts, layout, and assets, rather than missing packages on a provisioned host. A 50-pair validation of the S_pdf text-consistency metric achieves 94% precision for real content divergence. We release ETB, ETB-Porta, a recommender and portability gate, and a public cross-OS harness as shared infrastructure.

</details>


### [101] [One Gate Is Not Enough: Composing Stateful Pre-Action Controls for Agentic AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18360)
*Gaston Besanson*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文研究多重事前控制同时约束智能体行动时的“补救诱发控制耦合”问题：一个控制的补救措施可能改变其他控制重新评估所依赖的行动、证据或上下文，从而使其先前判定失效。作者提出“补救后重新设闸”协议，并通过形式化、有限模型检验与开放数据实验说明其必要性与边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多控制治理通常默认各控制的判定可独立复用，但证据替换、资源降级等补救操作会改写被审查对象或审查条件。若不重新执行受影响控制，系统可能在表面满足流程的情况下执行未经当前条件验证的行动。论文还关注治理证据缓冲区对最近一次已接纳写入的自信任风险，以及组合多个检测器并不会自动扩大检测覆盖范围的问题。

Method: 作者形式化定义补救诱发的控制耦合，并在有界、幂等且满足既定假设的场景下证明“补救后重新设闸”协议可恢复单次行动的健全性。针对已实现的证据替换和资源预算降路两类算子，使用有限模型检查搜索反例以检验交换性。论文还给出正权重线性聚合能够补偿成员否决的精确条件，构建跨控制统一证据集，并分析治理证据缓冲区的状态可信性；实证部分在确定性的开放数据工件上组合三个已发表引擎，以30个预注册随机种子检验CH1至CH6。

Result: 有限模型检查发现两种补救算子存在具体非交换反例，表明补救顺序属于控制平面语义而非单纯实现细节。重新设闸协议在论文限定条件下可保证每次行动的判定健全性。治理证据缓冲区即使仅信任自身最近一次接纳写入，仍可能因已声明但未覆盖的缺陷类别受到污染；两项缓解措施只能降低而不能消除风险。实验中，CH1至CH5在全部30个预注册种子下满足注册决策规则；CH6在W1工作流下满足，但在较小的W2工作流下不满足。

Conclusion: 论文的主要贡献是将补救操作视为会改变其他控制判定前提的语义事件，并要求对受影响控制进行重新评估。其证据支持：多控制组合的正确性依赖补救顺序、重新设闸和显式覆盖声明；检测器或证据机制的组合不能凭空产生新的检测能力。结论仅构成基于开放载荷数据和合成元数据层的机制演示，不应外推为真实生产环境中的普遍发生率或性能结论。

Abstract: Agentic AI systems take consequential actions governed by more than one pre-action control at once: authority, resource, and evidence gates that can admit, degrade, or remediate an action before it executes. This paper's central object is remediation-induced control coupling: a remediation applied by one control can change the action, evidence, or context another control evaluates, invalidating that control's earlier judgment. We formalize this coupling and give a remediate-and-regate protocol that restores per-action soundness in the current bounded, idempotent setting under its stated assumptions. We further show that the two implemented remediation operators (evidence substitution and resource-budget downroute) do not commute -- a finite-model checker finds concrete counterexample instances -- making remediation order part of the control-plane semantics rather than an implementation detail. A governed evidence buffer that trusts its own most recent admitted write is a further instance of the same problem at the level of state -- current admissibility does not imply future reference trustworthiness -- and is vulnerable to poisoning from declared-uncovered defect classes; two mitigations reduce, not eliminate, that exposure. Supporting results establish the exact condition under which positive-weight linear aggregation of gate outcomes can compensate a member veto, a unified cross-control Evidence Set, and that composition manufactures no new detection coverage, reported honestly. Empirically, on a deterministic open-data artifact composing three published engines unmodified, CH1-CH5 meet their registered decision rules across all 30 pre-registered seeds; CH6 does so under W1 but not under the smaller W2 workflow, reported as such. This is a mechanism demonstration on open payload data with a synthetic metadata layer, not a claim about production prevalence.

</details>


### [102] [Building real-time digital twin instances with Function+Data Flow: user evaluation and extension for iterative pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18480)
*Eduardo de Conto,Blaise Genest,Arvind Easwaran,Nicholas Ng,Shweta Menon*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文评估了用于人工智能数字孪生开发的可视化领域专用语言FDF及其集成环境DesCartes Builder。用户研究表明，该方案具有较好的可用性，尤其适合领域专家，并据此提出支持层次化、迭代式和模块化流程的H-FDF扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有人工智能与机器学习驱动的数字孪生管线通常以临时方式构建，存在难以规格化、验证和复用的问题，且缺少专用开发工具。研究旨在降低此类系统的开发门槛，并提升建模过程的可靠性与工程化程度。

Method: 研究以FDF为基础，利用DesCartes Builder让参与者实现一个具有代表性的实时数字孪生原型。通过定量与定性用户研究，测量参与者对系统可用性和功能充分性的感知，并收集工具及语言框架的优势、缺陷和改进建议。基于研究发现，作者进一步设计了H-FDF层次化扩展。

Result: 实验结果显示，DesCartes Builder与FDF在潜在用户群体中达到良好的可用性水平，对目标用户即领域专家尤其有效。研究还识别出工具交互、功能支持和框架表达能力方面的具体优点与待改进点。H-FDF被提出以表达更复杂的数字孪生管线，例如包含双重训练的流程。

Conclusion: FDF通过显式表示和组合机器学习功能，为人工智能数字孪生管线的规格化、复用与验证提供了基础；DesCartes Builder证明了集成式、模型驱动平台的实践可行性。未来应完善H-FDF及配套工具，以支持复杂、迭代和模块化的数字孪生开发流程。

Abstract: Digital twins (DTs) increasingly leverage artificial intelligence (AI) and machine learning (ML) pipelines, both to build real-time DTs from high-fidelity simulations and to instantiate them with historical data. However, engineering these pipelines remains largely ad-hoc: pipelines are hard to specify, validate, and reuse, with scarce dedicated tooling. Function+Data Flow (FDF) addresses this by defining a visual domain-specific language (DSL) that represents functions (ML models) explicitly, enabling their composition and reuse. We implemented FDF in DesCartes Builder, an integrated modeling environment supporting FDF-based DT synthesis and validation.
  In this paper, we report on an empirical user study evaluating whether FDF and DesCartes Builder can make AI-based DT development more accessible and reliable. Participants implemented a representative real-time DT prototype within DesCartes Builder, and we measured perceived usability and feature adequacy through quantitative and qualitative measures. Our results indicate that DesCartes Builder and FDF achieve a good level of usability across a broad range of potential users, and particularly for the intended audience of domain experts. The study additionally surfaces concrete strengths and areas for improvement of both the tool and the underlying FDF framework. Informed by these findings, we propose H-FDF, a Hierarchical extension of FDF supporting iterative and modular pipelines, enabling the formal specification of more complex DT pipelines such as dual training. Our findings suggest that integrated, model-driven platforms are a promising direction to transform AI-based DT engineering into a disciplined modeling practice.

</details>


### [103] [SemaPLC: A Project-Grounded, Verification-Gated Agent Harness for PLC Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18565)
*Yanlun Tu,Huacan Wang,Ziyue Zhou,Jie Zhou,Ningyan Zhu,Ge Chen,Wangyi Chen,Tengfei Zhou,Yifan Zhou,Dasheng Yang,Xiaofeng Mou,Hui Zhang,Yi Xu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出SemaPLC，一个面向PLC控制逻辑生成的项目级智能体框架，以外部可验证证据而非模型自评作为任务完成条件。在117个独立POU任务和65个真实项目上下文任务上，该方法在编译、静态行为及动态运行验证方面均取得最高平均表现，尤其显著提升运行时动态正确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型虽可生成独立PLC程序组织单元，但其能否无缝集成到既有工程并在实际运行环境中正确执行，尚缺乏充分验证。仅依赖静态评分或模型自身判断，无法可靠反映控制逻辑的真实执行效果。

Method: SemaPLC将常规工程工具组织为受严格验证门控约束的智能体流程。任务只有在日志化外部检查全部通过后才被判定完成：首先核验是否符合规格，其次检查工程编译，最后将生成逻辑与参考逻辑部署到真实PLC运行时，对比执行轨迹以评估动态行为。

Result: 在117个独立POU基准任务上，SemaPLC在全部7个模型配置中获得最高严格验证通过率，平均为72.6%。在65个要求逻辑嵌入真实工程并实际运行的任务上，其集成编译、静态行为和动态行为指标均居首。动态验证的区分度最大：各方法静态分数差距不超过10个百分点，但基线动态分数仅为22.4至31.4，而SemaPLC达到52.2。

Conclusion: PLC代码生成的可靠性应以真实执行验证为核心，而不应仅依据静态分析或自我评估。通过将规格、编译和运行时轨迹验证设为硬性完成门槛，SemaPLC能系统性提升项目级PLC逻辑生成质量，且对实际运行正确性的改善最为显著。

Abstract: Programmable logic controllers (PLCs) run industrial plants, and large language models can already generate independent program organization units (POUs) for them. Whether such logic integrates into an existing PLC project and then runs correctly has been checked only in limited tests. We present \textsc{SemaPLC}, a project-grounded and verification-gated agent harness assembled from conventional tools but governed by a strict completion rule. Rather than stopping when the model judges its own output adequate, \textsc{SemaPLC} declares a task complete only when logged external checks confirm it. Those checks cover the specification, the compilation, and the behavior on a live runtime. On 117 independent-POU tasks matching existing benchmarks, it attains the highest strict verified pass rate on all seven models (72.6\% mean). On a project-context track of 65 tasks whose generated logic must compile and run inside a real project, it attains the highest mean on integrated compilation, static behavior, and dynamic behavior. Of the three layers, dynamic behavior is the most revealing. We measure it by deploying the generated and the reference logic to a live PLC runtime and comparing their executed traces. All methods fall within 10 static points of one another, whereas dynamic scores separate them sharply, from 22.4 to 31.4 for the baselines against 52.2 for \textsc{SemaPLC}. Overall, our verification-gated harness raises the mean at every layer and most sharply at runtime. Execution, not static scoring, is the faithful test of whether generated control logic actually works. \textsc{SemaPLC} is open-sourced at https://github.com/midea-ai/SemaPLC.

</details>


### [104] [AppEval: A Unified Benchmark for LLM-Based Mobile Application Repair in ArkTS, Swift, and Kotlin](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18588)
*Bang Xie,Hao Liu,Zhenyu Shi,Yonghao Zhang,Senjian Zhang,Zhiyuan Peng,Xin Yin,Chenhao Ying,Yuan Luo,Wei Chen,Haiming Jin,Shaocong Long,Xu Liu,Zhe Peng*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出AppEval：一个面向HarmonyOS/ArkTS、iOS/Swift与Android/Kotlin移动应用修复的基准及原生工具链评测框架。其核心是以真实“构建—安装—启动—测试”链路验证补丁，并将基础设施异常与程序行为失败区分。当前经审计的量化结果仅覆盖Android：200个任务、24个可独立构建仓库，五个智能体的Pass@1为22.00%至90.50%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统仓库级LLM修复评测多假设测试可直接在构建主机运行，无法验证补丁在移动设备或模拟器上的实际可执行性。SDK缺失、设备离线、应用在断言前崩溃等问题可能被误判为程序测试失败，从而降低评测结果的可信度与可比性。

Method: AppEval为每个任务分离隐藏行为测试与参考生产修复，并要求同一已安装应用目标在缺陷版本上稳定到达断言失败、在补丁版本上通过测试；无法构建、安装、启动或连接测试设备等情况被单列为基础设施失败。框架通过统一模式将该验收契约映射至不同平台的构建系统、运行时和测试运行器。Android部分经过审计，筛选出200个满足动态验收条件的instrumentation任务。

Result: 在相同动态预言机下，五个LLM智能体在Android任务集上的Pass@1介于22.00%和90.50%之间，最高与最低相差68.50个百分点。该差异表明，移动应用修复能力对所评估智能体高度敏感；同时，严格的运行时验收能够避免把环境问题混入补丁正确性判断。

Conclusion: AppEval强调移动应用修复必须跨越构建、安装、启动与设备端测试的完整边界，才能形成有意义的修复评测。论文的实证结论目前只支持Android范围内的比较；在iOS和HarmonyOS上完成同等审计与量化评测之前，不应据此作出跨平台泛化结论。

Abstract: Repository-level LLM agents are typically evaluated on projects whose tests run on the build host. It remains unclear whether their repairs survive the mobile build-install-launch-test boundary, where a missing SDK, offline device, or pre-assertion crash can be mistaken for a program failure. We present AppEval, a benchmark and native-toolchain evaluation framework for mobile application repair across HarmonyOS/ArkTS, iOS/Swift, and Android/Kotlin. Each task separates a hidden behavior test from the reference production fix and is accepted only when the same installed-app target reaches an assertion failure on the defective revision and passes after the fix; infrastructure failures remain a distinct outcome. A common schema maps this contract to each platform's build system, runtime, and test runner. The audited Android partition contains 200 accepted instrumentation tasks from 24 independently buildable repositories. On these tasks, five agents achieve Pass@1 between 22.00% and 90.50%, a 68.50-percentage-point spread under the same dynamic oracle. These results show that mobile repair performance depends strongly on the evaluated agent while demonstrating why runtime-aware acceptance is necessary for meaningful comparison. The quantitative findings in this paper are Android-specific; audited iOS and HarmonyOS results are required before drawing cross-platform generalization conclusions.

</details>


### [105] [OdinEval: A Reproducible Benchmark for LLM-Based Program Repair in the Odin Programming Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18595)
*Bang Xie,Hao Liu,Zhiyuan Peng,Xin Yin,Senjian Zhang,Yuan Luo,Chenhao Ying,Haiming Jin,Wei Chen,Shaocong Long,Zhenyu Shi*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出OdinEval：一个面向Odin系统编程语言的可复现实验级代码修复基准。其核心贡献是将缺陷议题、修复前后提交、标准补丁、回归测试、历史工具链与执行记录绑定，并在168个严格筛选的实例上统一评测六个语言模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有仓库级自动程序修复基准主要覆盖少数主流语言，系统语言Odin的评测不足，导致模型在该生态中的真实修复能力缺少可靠比较。作者旨在构建具有可追溯性、可执行性和严格正确性验证的Odin专用基准，以补足这一空白。

Method: 每个实例关联缺陷议题、基线提交、修复提交、标准补丁、针对该议题的回归测试、历史版本工具链及运行记录。实例准入条件为：回归测试必须在修复前版本失败，并在标准修复后通过。若缺少可用的开发者测试，则构造黑盒测试，并经同一模型的三个独立实例审查；测试需分别在两个历史状态执行，并依据已记录反馈在版本化的Test Writing Skill流程中修订。随后，作者以统一协议在168个过滤后的实例上评估六个语言模型，并报告Resolved与Repro等指标。

Result: 在168个实例的统一评测中，Kimi-K3取得最高Resolved得分，为66.7%，表明其生成的修复通过验证的比例最高；Qwen3.8-Max取得最高Repro得分，为96.4%，表明其复现或满足基准执行要求的能力最强。基准发布内容包括冻结数据、源码归档、容器、验证器、模型补丁与审计清单。

Conclusion: OdinEval通过提交级缺陷溯源、历史环境固定、回归测试双状态验证和审计材料发布，提供了较强可复现性的Odin仓库级修复评测基础设施。结果显示，不同模型在最终修复成功率与复现能力上存在明显差异；该基准可用于评估和推动面向非主流系统语言的自动修复研究。

Abstract: Repository-level repair benchmarks still center on a few mainstream languages, leaving systems languages such as Odin largely untested. We present OdinEval, a reproducible benchmark built from documented defects in public Odin repositories. Each instance binds an issue to base and fix commits, a gold patch, an issue-specific regression test, a historical toolchain, and execution records. Admission requires the test to fail on the base revision and pass after the gold fix. When no usable developer test exists, a black-box test is reviewed independently by three instances of the same model, executed in both historical states, and revised from recorded feedback under a versioned Test Writing Skill. We evaluate six language models on 168 filtered instances under one shared protocol. Kimi-K3 records the highest Resolved score at 66.7%, while Qwen3.8-Max has the highest Repro score at 96.4%. The release includes frozen data, source archives, containers, validators, model patches, and audit manifests.

</details>


### [106] [Code Health in LLM-Based Test Generation: Effectiveness and Token Efficiency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18645)
*Freya Wirdemann,Markus Borg,Nadim Hagatulah,Adam Tornhill*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文考察代码可维护性与大语言模型生成单元测试效果的关系。结果表明，CodeScene的CodeHealth（CH）对测试有效性具有较弱但稳定的预测信号，并且CH越高，代码转换后的输入Token数量通常越少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有研究显示，高质量、易维护的代码更利于AI软件工程工具发挥作用，但代码可维护性是否会系统性影响LLM生成单元测试的质量仍缺少实证证据。论文旨在补充这一关联，并分析其可能的Token层面机制。

Method: 研究以CodeScene的CH量化代码可维护性，在Python、Java和C++代码上评估LLM生成单元测试。测试有效性通过传统代码覆盖率指标及变异得分衡量；同时使用常见工业Tokenizer统计不同CH水平代码对应的输入Token数量，并分析CH与上述指标的相关关系。

Result: CH与LLM生成测试的有效性之间呈现弱但一致的关联：较高可维护性通常对应更好的覆盖或变异测试表现，但效应强度有限。CH还与输入Token数量负相关，即可维护性较高的代码一般需要更少的Token表示。该趋势在所研究的多种编程语言和评估维度中具有一致性。

Conclusion: 代码可维护性是影响LLM辅助测试生成效果的一个有用但非决定性因素。CH可作为评估LLM测试生成难度和预期效果的辅助信号；同时，较高可维护性伴随较低Token负担，说明代码质量可能通过改善上下文表示效率而有利于LLM软件开发任务。

Abstract: Coding agents powered by Large Language Models (LLMs) are now prominent in software engineering. Previous work has shown that AI tools perform better on high-quality source code that is easy to maintain. In this study, we investigate how the effectiveness of LLM-generated unit tests varies across maintainability levels measured by CodeScene's CodeHealth (CH). We assess test effectiveness using traditional coverage metrics and mutation score across Python, Java, and C++. Moreover, we study how code with different levels of CH translates into input tokens using common industrial tokenizers. Our results suggest that CH provides a weak but consistent signal of LLM-generated test effectiveness and is negatively correlated with input-token count. These findings provide further evidence for a relationship between maintainability and LLM-based software development.

</details>


### [107] [Flama: a Python framework for development and deployment of production-ready APIs, machine learning, and LLM services](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18733)
*José A. Perdiguero López,Miguel A. Durán-Olivencia*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文介绍了 Flama：一个基于 ASGI 的开源 Python 框架，用统一的异步、类型驱动架构，同时支持 Web API、传统机器学习模型服务与大语言模型应用的开发和部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 API 框架、模型服务平台和大语言模型推理引擎通常相互割裂，开发者需组合多套工具链，并面对不同的模型封装、推理后端、通信协议和部署方式。Flama 的目标是降低这类异构系统的集成与生产部署复杂度。

Method: Flama 由七个核心子系统构成：基于类型注解的组件化依赖注入；兼容 Pydantic、Marshmallow、Typesystem 的统一模式适配层；基于 SQLAlchemy、Repository 与 Unit of Work 模式的自动 CRUD 生成器；面向多类机器学习框架的 .flm 模型二进制封装格式；支持 vLLM 与 MLX、并兼容多种接口协议的 LLM 服务层；经由 Maturin 编译的 Rust 加速核心；以及基于 JSON-RPC 2.0 的 MCP 服务模块。框架还集成认证、分页、后台任务、WebSocket、流式响应、OpenAPI 自动生成和命令行工具。

Result: 摘要主要陈述系统设计与功能覆盖范围，未提供定量实验、性能基准或实际部署指标。其宣称的成果是：以单一架构覆盖 REST 开发、预测模型部署和生成式推理，并提供跨模型框架、跨 LLM 后端及跨客户端协议的统一接入能力。

Conclusion: Flama 定位为面向生产环境的一体化 Python 服务框架，其差异化在于将类型驱动 API 开发、传统模型服务、LLM 推理协议适配、Rust 性能优化和 MCP 集成纳入统一体系。该摘要表明论文以架构说明、示例和与现有方案的比较为主；框架实际优势仍需依赖吞吐、延迟、可扩展性、兼容性及运维成本等实证结果进一步验证。

Abstract: We present Flama, an open-source Python framework for developing and deploying production-ready web APIs, machine learning services, and large-language-model (LLM) applications. Built on the Asynchronous Server Gateway Interface (ASGI), Flama offers a type-driven, async-first programming model that unifies REST API development, predictive model serving, and generative AI inference in one architecture.
  It is organised around seven subsystems: a component-based dependency injection system resolving handler parameters from type annotations at startup; a pluggable schema layer supporting Pydantic, Marshmallow and Typesystem behind a single adapter; an automatic CRUD generator turning a SQLAlchemy table and a schema class into REST endpoints backed by the Repository and Unit of Work patterns; a portable binary format (.flm) packaging models from scikit-learn, TensorFlow, PyTorch and Hugging Face Transformers with their metadata for zero-code deployment; a multi-backend LLM server running vLLM (Linux/CUDA) or MLX (Apple Silicon) and exposing four wire protocols (OpenAI, Anthropic, Ollama, and a native streaming dialect) through a shared codec; a Rust-accelerated core compiled via Maturin for routing, JSON encoding, compression and parsing; and a Model Context Protocol module turning any application into an MCP server over JSON-RPC 2.0.
  Built-in capabilities include JWT authentication, two pagination strategies, background tasks in threads or processes, WebSocket endpoints, Server-Sent Event and NDJSON streaming, OpenAPI 3.2.0 generation from handler signatures, and a command-line interface for running applications and for serving, packaging and inspecting models.
  We describe the architecture, present the programming model through worked examples, and compare Flama with existing frameworks, model serving platforms and LLM inference engines.

</details>


### [108] [Contract-Aware Rescue of a Drifted Isabelle Development: The Double-Tank Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18822)
*Jim Woodcock,Gabriel Leite,Augusto Sampaio,Ran Wei*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文指出：交互式定理证明器中的“构建成功”不足以证明原验证任务未被悄然改变。作者以 Isabelle 中的采样数据双水箱控制器为例，使用契约感知工具 CAPRI 对证明修复过程施加变更约束，最终在原始九个理论文件结构内完成十项既定证明义务；但运行层面的端到端验证仍未完成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型能够生成可被证明器接受的证明，但在修复过程中，定理陈述、假设、文件结构或验证目标可能被改写或弱化。仅依赖 Isabelle 的类型检查和证明检查，无法保证修复后的仓库仍忠实于原始验证意图，因此需要可审计地约束允许的编辑范围与语义影响。

Method: 作者首先回溯审计一个已扩展至16个理论文件、表面上无 sorry、oops、额外公理化和证明器特殊信任机制使用的版本。审计记录100个原始声明中的实质性变更，并识别稳定与失效证明状态。随后采用 CAPRI，将 Isabelle 的构建接受性与针对仓库修改的机器可读编辑契约检查结合，按原始九理论文件范围重建证明；此外由共同作者进行二次回放，核验构建、契约检查、控制测试及主要审计发现。

Result: 回溯审计发现，100个原始声明中有16个发生实质改变；其中一个端到端保证定理被弱化为在结论包含的四项需求中预先假设三项。经 CAPRI 约束的重建在原始九理论文件结构中解决了全部10项限定证明义务。共同作者成功复现 R10 构建、契约检查、控制测试和主要审计结论，但尚未完成独立第三方复现。

Conclusion: 形式化证明的可接受性不能替代对验证任务完整性的确认。CAPRI 所代表的“证明器检查加编辑契约审计”方法可降低证明修复中目标漂移和保证被弱化的风险，并能支持可复查的重建过程。不过，该案例尚未把实际运行执行与重建后的定量轨迹契约连接起来；要获得完整的运行级端到端保证，仍需制定并验证扩展契约。

Abstract: Large language models can propose proofs for interactive theorem provers, but a successful build does not show the surrounding verification task was preserved. We study this problem in an Isabelle development of a sampled-data double-tank controller. The work began with nine theories and ten unfinished obligations, grew to a 16-theory build without sorry, oops, added axiomatisation, or oracle use, and accumulated 23 stable and 36 broken proof states. A retrospective audit found material changes in 16 of the 100 original declarations, including a weakened end-to-end assurance theorem that assumed three of the four requirements in its conclusion. We used CAPRI, a contract-aware proof-repair tool, to govern a reconstruction by combining Isabelle acceptance with an independent check of repository changes against machine-readable edit contracts. The reconstruction discharged all ten scoped obligations within the original nine-theory structure. A secondary replay by a co-author reproduced the R10 build, contract checks, control tests, and principal audit findings; independent replication remains future work. Operational end-to-end verification remains incomplete: we still need to connect operational executions to the reconstructed quantitative trace contract, a task requiring an extended contract.

</details>


### [109] [SkillForge: Self-Distilling Agents for Project-Specific Issue Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18933)
*Silin Chen,Han Li,Xiaodong Gu,Yuling Shi,Haibing Guan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出SkillForge，一种面向代码仓库的主动式自蒸馏框架。它通过从仓库中合成与核心功能对应的问题并完成修复，预先提炼可复用的项目专属技能，从而提升大语言模型智能体处理真实软件问题的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有软件修复智能体虽具备较强通用能力，但缺少特定仓库的领域知识。依赖历史问题记录的方法受限于可用的历史修复信号，而在线探索式方法又会为每个新问题带来较高测试时成本。因此，需要一种无需等待真实问题出现、且能低成本积累项目知识的机制。

Method: SkillForge以测试覆盖的仓库核心功能为基础，重新实现并据此构造项目专属的合成问题；智能体解决这些合成问题后，框架将过程中获得的知识蒸馏为与代码实体绑定的技能，并建立技能与相关仓库实体之间的关联。在后续真实问题处理时，智能体可检索和利用这些实体落地的项目知识。

Result: 在采用开源模型与闭源模型的实验中，SkillForge相较于强基线方法均能稳定提高软件问题解决性能。摘要未提供具体数据集、评估指标或提升幅度，因此无法进一步量化其收益。

Conclusion: 主动从代码仓库自身生成训练信号，并在真实问题到来前积累实体级项目知识，是增强软件修复智能体仓库适应性的有效路径。SkillForge的核心贡献在于以合成问题替代对历史修复轨迹和昂贵在线探索的依赖。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) based agents have demonstrated remarkable proficiency in automated software issue resolution, yet they often struggle to resolve issues in a specific repository because they lack project-specific knowledge. Existing self-evolving approaches acquire such knowledge from repository history or online repair trajectories, but they either depend on available historical issue-resolution signals or incur substantial per-issue test-time exploration cost. In this paper, we propose SkillForge, a self-distillation framework that proactively acquires project-specific knowledge from the repository itself. Instead of waiting for real issues to expose project-specific knowledge gaps, SkillForge synthesizes project-specific issues by re-implementing test-covered core functionalities of the repository. By resolving these synthetic issues, SkillForge distills reusable project-specific knowledge into entity-grounded skills and associates them with relevant repository entities for future issue resolution. Extensive experiments using both open-source and closed-source models show that SkillForge consistently improves issue resolution performance over strong baselines. These results demonstrate that proactively acquiring project-specific knowledge before solving real issues substantially improves downstream software issue resolution.

</details>
